<p> Assistant Administrator for Economic Growth , Education and Environment , United States Agency for International Development <p> Confirmed <p> Of 743 key positions requiring Senate confirmation ... <p> 136 <p> No nominee <p> 12 <p> Awaiting nomination <p> 83 <p> Formally nominated <p> 515 <p> Confirmed <p> The Post and Partnership for Public Service , a nonprofit , nonpartisan organization , are tracking roughly 700 key executive branch nominations through the confirmation process . These positions include Cabinet secretaries , deputy and assistant secretaries , chief financial officers , general counsel , heads of agencies , ambassadors and other critical leadership positions . These are a portion of the roughly 1,200 positions that require Senate confirmation . <p> The Senate can only act on nominations that have been formally submitted by the Trump administration . Those marked " awaiting nomination " above have been announced but not yet submitted , while those marked " formally nominated " are awaiting action by the Senate . <h> Share of key positions filled , by agency <p> Filled <p> Nomination pending Senate approval <p> A <p> Acting secretary <p> Energy <p> 87% <p> Veterans Affairs <p> 83% <p> Defense <p> 79% <p> State <p> 79% <p> Health and Human Services <p> 72% <p> Labor <p> 71% <p> Housing and Urban Development <p> 69% <p> Treasury <p> 68% <p> Interior <p> 67% <p> Education <p> 63% <p> Agriculture <p> 62% <p> Commerce <p> 62% <p> Transportation <p> 57% <p> Environmental Protection Agency <p> 57% <p> Justice <p> 48% <p> Homeland Security A <p> 47% <p> Note : Cabinet-level departments with 12 or more tracked positions are included . <p> Serves as chief foreign policy adviser , foreign policy coordinator , senior negotiator and senior Cabinet officer , and leads the department , which had nearly $26.5 billion in budget outlays in fiscal 2015 , and 10,500 nonseasonal full time permanent employees in fiscal 2016 <p> Nomination timeline <p> March 13 , 2018 <p> Appointment announced <p> March 20 , 2018 <p> Referred to Foreign RelationsB committee <p> April 23 , 2018 <p> Reported out favorably <p> April 26 , 2018 <p> Confirmed <p> Michael Pompeo <p> Details <p> ConfirmedDetails <p> John J.
